## 2026-11-23 — I19.04 FP-032 generated provider image URL provenance (NEW CANDIDATE)

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 104/132 completed**, unchanged. Prior source+docs head [`71c3e69480a0ace34a9f3ca23855e50cc73fb49e`](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/commit/71c3e69480a0ace34a9f3ca23855e50cc73fb49e) [CI #45095881583](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45095881583) **SUCCESS** (`validate`, CodeQL and Unified). FP-031 recurring/provider same-turn duplicate upload guard was previously accepted with [real browser #45095500141](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45095500141). PR #654 remains Draft→develop, source dependency bot PRs #657/#658/#660 and production release Draft #645 open.

**New confirmed contract mismatch:** Server `server/accountMetadataStore.ts` generates keys `<provider>-asset-<12hex>`, stores new provider artwork at `financial-provider-assets/providers/<provider>/<assetKey>.<mime-extension>`, and returns the derived public `/storage/v1/object/public/financial-provider-assets/providers/...?...v` URL. `src/lib/financialProviderClient.ts` accepted otherwise well-formed 200 receipts with an entirely unrelated HTTPS URL and `providerCatalogMatchesWrite` accepted such a URL from its purportedly final full-catalog GET when role, MIME, size and key otherwise matched. A newly uploaded binary could thus falsely appear fully saved even when its browser image referenced another bucket, provider directory, key or MIME. This is *not* a license to reject or migrate curated legacy provider URLs, which are independent read-only catalog metadata.

**Bounded candidate:** Require the exact new-server-asset public Storage path, provider directory, key and MIME extension both at positive upload acknowledgement and at aggregate catalog readback **only for expected freshly uploaded keys**. Preserve existing allowed HTTPS/loopback hosts, legacy-curated catalog data, same-owner AAL2/RLS, app cache/unknown-write protection, multi-slot image reuse, idempotent assignment and the independent metadata POST acknowledgement contract. Add direct client negative cases for otherwise perfect 200 receipts pointing at wrong provider/key/bucket/MIME/extra nested path/unrelated legacy URL; add exact binary catalog URL negative cases while keeping existing source integrity assertions. Correct valid QA synthetic assets to follow actual server path and serve only those synthetic Storage paths via the provider Chromium runner's intercepted image requests. Transform the existing synthetic provider **HTTP-200 ambiguous binary** scenario to have valid key/role/bytes and a **foreign provider Storage URL**; the editor must retain its accessible warning, disabled repeat Save, zero binding writes and no false green, in desktop and mobile.

**Validation PENDING:** New exact-head `validate`, CodeQL, Unified, one targeted existing `<!-- run-649-provider-receipt-qa -->` provider branding/receipt Chromium suite only after source green, download and direct inspect evidence, then remove the marker and update branch checkpoint. No new SQL migration, hosted Storage/DB write/delete, Android, release or merge. Original I19.04 scope still incomplete; no counters advanced.

## 2026-11-23 — FP-031 I19.04 recurring/provider same-turn generated-asset upload race candidate

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 104/132 completed** (unchanged; broad I19.04/I22.02 not claimed complete). Previous exact docs head `3df51c834d8e74e0e44cf1fe274cd298e340afac` [CI #45093794579](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45093794579) **SUCCESS** (`validate`, CodeQL and Unified); earlier combined dependency boundary isolated [Real Stack #45092659037](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45092659037) and focused [shared controls #45093220360](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45093220360)/[desktop backup #45093220381](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45093220381) **SUCCESS** on accepted `0f1553d`. All PR-body QA markers are OFF. Do not rerun previously accepted expensive gates without a new boundary.

**Confirmed source-level P0 race:** `RecurringPage.save` depended on React `editBusy` state rather than a synchronous ref; two user Save clicks before repaint could both pass validation and launch separate `uploadRecurringServiceAsset` requests and finance saves. The *exported clients* `uploadRecurringServiceAsset` (random `service-asset-` key) and `uploadFinancialProviderAsset` (random `provider-asset-` key) likewise admitted simultaneous PUTs to the **same** recurring item or provider/role/variant slot, despite the provider page's own accepted ref latch. This could create duplicate registered binaries and orphan cleanup ambiguity even while the final finance revision has not acknowledged. An existing post-response uncertainty lock in the recurring client alone does **not** prevent two concurrent calls before the first response.

**Coherent source fix and regression proof staged (candidate, not yet CI-accepted):** Add synchronous per-recurring-item in-flight protection to recurring binary client and per-provider/role/variant protection to provider client. A second concurrent upload receives explicit in-progress error and **does not issue a second PUT**; genuinely different targets remain independent. Keep the lock through initial PUT **and catalog readback**, release on a settled same-scope result, and clear at auth-scope reset. An old-scope request's finally must not unlock the new auth scope. Preserve all previous 0/408/429/5xx uncertain receipt, safe 400 retry, direct owner/AAL2/RLS, catalog provenance, finance revision and explicit cleanup contracts. Add `RecurringPage` synchronous `editSaveInFlight` ref before the first async Storage or finance writer; release in its existing `finally`, preserving existing modal/busy/error copy. Extend **existing** provider/recurring Vitest suites with same-target no-second-PUT, distinct-target progress, definite-400 retry and retired-auth-scope-late-completion negatives. Extend existing finance asset source test with latch-order assertion. Add actual in-browser same-task **two-click recurring Save** to the existing recurring-service-brand Chromium suite, asserting the one-asset replacement remains **exactly one PUT** and preserves old asset cleanup/payment propagation. Register FP-031 prevention pattern; no new duplicate QA suite or parallel style owner.

**Validation:** New candidate requires exact-head normal `validate`, JS/TS CodeQL, Unified and **one** opt-in `<!-- run-649-recurring-asset-qa -->` focused Chromium once source green; inspect actual PNG/logs before accepting this user-visible race guard. Direct hosted/production PostgreSQL/Storage mutations, SQL migration, Android, release, main/develop merge, full Windows/WebKit/Lighthouse 216-image visual are **NOT** part of this candidate. Keep PR #654 Draft → develop. Counters unchanged. Next: inspect new CI, fix only evidenced failures in one coherent corrective batch, run one targeted rendered QA, remove the opt-in marker and reconcile acceptance; then proceed to remaining original #649 P0/P1.

## 2026-11-22 — #649 I19.04 Storage POST uncertain-receipt byte-verified recovery (candidate)

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 104/132 completed**, unchanged. Previous accepted schema [`afd871d`](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/commit/afd871dd107897d1a84ff407203bdb070634210f) passed [CI #45071784608](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45071784608) and [isolated Real Stack #45071805623](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45071805623), both **SUCCESS**; the docs-only acceptance checkpoint [`53353a8`](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/commit/53353a89df24ac1a696bb278a0d18ac527f78018) passed [CI #45073447173](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45073447173) **SUCCESS**. Schema opt-in remains OFF; no repeat accepted Real Stack.

**New concrete I19.04 source gap:** `uploadFinancialProviderAsset` and sibling `uploadRecurringServiceAsset` each issue a one-time Storage `POST`, then a separate PostgreSQL registration RPC. Existing registration 5xx/lost-acknowledgement reconciliation protects committed binary references, but Storage POST itself could time out/return 5xx **after** persisting a real object. Previously the server returned a generic upstream failure without checking this first partial success; the client already blocks blind retry, but there was no safe path to recover the exact uploaded binary before registration. This is a distinct first-store ambiguous-receipt case, not a reason to weaken RLS or delete old keys.

**Proposed bounded server/cheap regression:** Reuse one shared `confirmedAmbiguousArtworkUpload` helper for the two existing public artwork buckets only. On Storage POST 5xx, timeout, 408/429, GET the **same server-generated random path** once (no repeat POST) using the same owner JWT/public API key; stream bytes with a 12-second whole-response timeout, cap at the original upload length, and require exact SHA-256 plus byte count before invoking the original registration RPC exactly once. A missing/truncated/mismatched/oversized/unreadable readback returns explicit `PROVIDER_ASSET_UPLOAD_UNVERIFIED` or `RECURRING_SERVICE_ASSET_UPLOAD_UNVERIFIED` (503); no PostgreSQL registration, Storage DELETE, shared key reuse, service-role key, raw error detail or client fake success. Deterministic Storage 4xx/auth validation still follows existing fail-closed behavior. Existing separate registration read-back, revision semantics, local receivable/Storage RLS and app UI uncertainty blocks remain untouched. Extend original provider/recurring unit tests with actual bytes/503 and failure cases, not a parallel suite. **Validation pending** on new code head; no claim of success until exact CI/CodeQL/Unified is observed. No DB migration or visual modification. Android, production, main and hosted Storage untouched.

**Next:** Verify source CI, correct only actual failures, then choose the next real unverified I19.04/P1 issue; defer already accepted full isolated DB and final Windows/WebKit/rendered matrix until coherent Ready head. PR #654 stays Draft → develop.

**New distinct I19.04/I22.02 direct-table lifecycle gap:** The approved owner+AAL2 `rheomiq_release_recurring_service_asset` SECURITY INVOKER RPC rejects deleting an image still linked by private relational recurring rows or any canonical finance `recurringOverrides`, `recurringCustom` or seed recurring references. **Proposed non-destructive SQL/source fix:** `20261122214000_guard_recurring_asset_direct_lifecycle.sql` installs an invoker table trigger for authenticated UPDATE/DELETE only: immutable registration identity/path metadata after creation; forbid reactivation of released rows; require active row release before deletion; enforce the same four read-only in-use checks as the already-approved release RPC before deactivation/deletion. Never rewrites, deletes or backfills historical data; the local service role remains explicitly outside browser-authenticated DML guards for disposable cleanup and owner-approved offline recovery. Existing normal two-phase release/purge remains supported and validated by the same established Real Stack end-to-end path.

**Real runtime candidate:** Extend the **existing** real GoTrue+Chromium `recurring-service-asset-durability` stage only, immediately after a true versioned finance save references an actual Storage-backed recurring image. Via direct actual owner AAL2 PostgREST, reject (a) PATCH `active=false` while referenced with `23503`, (b) PATCH recurring ID with `23514`, and (c) DELETE of active image with `23514`. Require exact service-role registry row and `updated_at` equality and unchanged Storage object count; then retain existing stale-revision, replacement, legitimate release/purge, source finance and cleanup cases to prove no regression. Existing source regression and strict release-pending migration inventory extended, no parallel suite.

**Acceptance pending:** exact new-head ordinary CI (validate/CodeQL/Unified) plus **one** isolated Real Stack for new trigger and complete legitimate finance/Storage lifecycle, with actual logs/artifacts when relevant. ## 2026-11-22 — #649 I19.04 receipt preflight must precede generic finance-dispatch ambiguity guard (correction candidate)

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 104/132 completed** (unchanged; no full I19.04 increment). On prior [`b4d3503`](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/commit/b4d350357bb6134fb24048c6e8aea7cc94fe002b), [focused Chromium #45037535921](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/45037535921) **FAILED in the new stale-prefill scenario** after the original OCR, actual post-commit lost-delete/reload, local cleanup and duplicate-purchase association paths had passed. The injected second-tab `posted` marker was committed and the real Quick Entry submit clicked, but no specific stale-draft form error appeared. Source diagnosis: `QuickAdd.submit` sets `financeDispatched=true` before invoking its `onCreate` callback; a callback-local preflight rejection therefore activates the **correct but inappropriate-for-this-precommit-case** `FINANCE_RECEIPT_UNVERIFIED` safety warning rather than the actionable local status error. Do not remove the unverified receipt guard or classify an arbitrary failed finance callback as a guaranteed rollback; this is an invocation-order root cause, not an uncertain server response.

**Scoped shared-owner fix:** Introduce optional `onBeforeFinanceWrite?:()=>Promise<void>` in the existing generic `QuickAdd`; invoke it *inside QuickAdd's own try* after form/domain validation and **before the first `financeDispatched=true`**, for both single and linked-transfer branches. `ReceiptAwareQuickAdd` supplies an async callback that calls the existing `assertReceiptDraftUnposted` only when a receipt draft ID is attached. Remove the same preflight from the post-dispatch `completeWithReceipt` callback. **Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 101/132 completed** (unchanged; I19.04/I22.02 and remaining P0/P1 complete packages not yet accepted). Previous exact [`151618d`](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/commit/151618d383751d6dbef7783beb3f24a19b5867c6) passed [CI #44954811808](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/44954811808) **SUCCESS** (validate, CodeQL, Unified) including the provider create/update postcommit receipt recovery. **Evidence-backed security gap:** Production-applied `20260904193923_add_financial_provider_registry.sql` grants provider catalog `SELECT` to **any authenticated** token (`using (true)`), and `20260905004603_add_financial_provider_assets.sql` grants active artwork metadata `SELECT` to any authenticated token (`using (active = true)`). Later `20261001192135_manage_financial_provider_assets.sql` restricts only writes and slot bindings to owner+AAL2, leaving a direct PostgREST read bypass of API-level owner checks for owner-edited provider display/short names and asset filenames. Existing single-owner semantics require the same live owner UID, AAL2, active device and app-lock scope on direct metadata reads.

**Staged forward-only SQL fix:** `supabase/migrations/20261122080000_restrict_provider_catalog_to_owner_aal2.sql` replaces only those two permissive SELECT policies with `(select public.rheomiq_is_owner_aal2())`, preserving `active=true` filtering for artworks, authenticated owner SELECT grants, metadata rows, existing write/binding/Storage policies and canonical finance history. Strict release-pending migration-ledger tracking and new contract assertion were added. The existing disposable `scripts/app-lock-local-postgres-smoke.sh` now models the original broad SELECT policies, applies the **actual** new migration, asserts both provider and artwork positive read access for owner+AAL2 before lock and after verified ticket, and zero visibility after PIN lock and for owner AAL1, nonowner AAL2 and revoked owner AAL2. This local PostgreSQL test uses only synthetic rows and claims in a rolled-back transaction, not hosted/production data.

**Acceptance pending on candidate HEAD:** Run real CI `validate` (including local PostgreSQL SQL execution), CodeQL/Unified and one explicit disposable real-stack head to verify existing authenticated catalog/API/Chromium behavior is not broken. The SQL smoke proves row-level negative policy with synthetic local identities; do not mislabel it as a genuine GoTrue nonowner direct read test. ## 2026-11-19 — #649 I22.02/I19.04 auth-scope metadata cache invalidation + focused recurring rendered QA selector

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 101/132 completed** (unchanged; original I22.02 session/RLS coverage and cross-store I19.04 remain partial). Prior exact [CI #44601984407](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/44601984407) at `c3521d5` **SUCCESS** for validate, CodeQL and Unified; recurring GET/upload/delete strict readbacks source-accepted.

**Confirmed cross-session race:** The three independent long-lived browser modules (`accountMetadataClient`, `financialProviderClient`, `recurringServiceAssetClient`) cached owner-scoped metadata after logout, MFA downgrade or subsequent successful sign-in. More seriously, an older pending owner-AAL2 GET could fulfill *after* a scope boundary and repopulate IBANs/custom provider names/image entries into a new anonymous/MFA context. A shared `authScopedCache.ts` registers four existing/new auth-boundary event sources and a monotonic request epoch. On a real session reset the stores immediately clear sensitive state (provider resets to built-in fallback), invalidate previous pending GETs and require an authenticated fresh read. Pending old GET responses cannot overwrite newer records. IBAN and recurring asset PUT/DELETE also refuse to publish success from a pre-boundary operation; no duplicate uploads, destructive deletes, direct production Auth mutation or token disclosure. `useSession` dispatches a generic nonsecret reset on genuine unauthenticated session/401/successful logout; prior auth-expired/MFA-required/auth-completed signaling and MFA/AAL2 guards retained.

**Focused verification:** New `tests/649-auth-scoped-metadata-caches.test.ts` exercises four auth boundaries × three independent stale-inflight GET stores, including reset before an old response resolves and acceptance of a later current GET, plus source guard on logout and QA trigger. It is not a full screenshot/Windows/WebKit gate.

**Validation / boundaries:** This candidate requires its own exact-head mandatory source, CodeQL and Unified check, then targeted recurring-service rendered screenshots/log inspection; browser session/expiry negative states have earlier accepted I03.03 QA but final Ready rendered/Windows/WebKit/Lighthouse still pending. ## 2026-11-19 — #649 I19.04 batch: provider RPC exact receipts, binding GET verification, IBAN cold/stale readback preflight

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 101/132 completed** (unchanged: I19.04 remains PARTIAL; no double count). Preceding exact [CI #44586310982](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/44586310982) at `2a1902e` **SUCCESS**: 327 Vitest suites/2052 tests, TypeScript/build/privacy/bundle, disposable PostgreSQL, CodeQL and Unified. Single implementation PR #654 Draft → develop; archived #650/#651/#653 and separate issues #646/#648/#652 already closed by owner-requested consolidation.

**Confirmed related false-green boundaries:**
1. `writeProviderRpc` previously returned the first row from any nonempty PostgREST RPC reply without comparing the actual ID/display/shortName/type/country/order against the submitted create/update. HTTP 200 with the wrong or duplicated row could be treated as a confirmed persistence receipt. It now requires exactly one row and exact submitted fields; otherwise **503 FINANCIAL_PROVIDER_WRITE_UNVERIFIED** (and malformed records retain strict schema failure). There is no automatic replay of a non-idempotent create.
2. The `rheomiq_set_financial_provider_asset_binding` RPC **returns void** and a plain HTTP 200/null is insufficient confirmation of a specific slot. Server now reads exactly that provider/role/variant via authenticated RLS after the RPC and requires the matching key (or no row for an explicit clear) before reporting `ok:true` to clients. Malformed/duplicate/unavailable readback becomes an uncertain failed receipt, not a green save.
3. Browser IBAN `saveAccountMetadata` previously used revision zero on a cold/failed account catalog, potentially causing a partial finance/metadata write; it now performs one bounded, read-only GET preflight when snapshot is cold/loading/error. If readback remains unverified, no PUT is issued. Verified existing revision is used in If-Match, and the prior exact server/client write receipt guard remains active.

**Regression:** New real mocked provider-RPC and binding-RPC+GET suite: eleven provider create/update/malformed/duplicate fixtures and eight binding-assignment/clear/ambiguity/unavailable fixtures. Expanded IBAN browser client scenarios: cold GET, stale/failed GET never POSTs and current revision 4 produces If-Match 4/next revision 5. These are focused I19.04 server/client finance-adjacent changes, not UI redesign, migrations or a new SQL function. No live hosted Supabase or Storage mutation, local/private finance data, main, release, Android or develop merge.

**Validation:** New candidate needs its own exact-head source `validate`, CodeQL and Unified result; previously successful head cannot be used to claim acceptance. Draft targeted suites/ordinary CI are sufficient for this source-only batch; defer Windows/WebKit/Lighthouse/full rendered/real stack to final coherent Ready head and verify relevant failure/recovery states there. I18.02 full independent PostgreSQL+Storage restore remains owner-approval-gated, #657/#658/#660 independent dependency work. ## 2026-11-19 — I18.05 connected device same-turn mutation latch and stale read suppression (source/browser candidate)

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 99/132 completed (unchanged, I18.05 not yet accepted).** Exact previous [CI #44545256452](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/44545256452) at `ef1e19f` **SUCCESS** (validate, CodeQL, Unified required gate, strict budget, local PostgreSQL). No other branch owners were changed.

**Confirmed interaction race:** `DeviceAccessSettings` relied exclusively on asynchronous `setBusy(true)` to disable follow-up confirmations. A second confirm event in the same synchronous turn could trigger an independent duplicate revoke/rename call: the later 404 or failed response could overwrite the genuine outcome. The owner-controlled remote session may then look unrevoked even after a successful server change. Introduce a synchronous `useRef` mutation latch around revoke-one, revoke-others and nickname saves; keep all existing owner/AAL2/Origin and actual server authorization unchanged. Use a request generation counter so a GET read started before mutation cannot overwrite its post-mutation acknowledged device list or display old read errors. Invalidate any outstanding GET and clear its loading spinner when starting mutation; preserve accurate red errors and only show green after verified mutation + resulting list.

**Targeted regression:** existing `tests/device-access.test.ts` protects latch, read-order and receipt rules. Existing `scripts/settings-tabs-qa.mjs` desktop synthetic device-revoke error journey now double-clicks the confirmation in the same JS task, and asserts exactly **one** POST through QA-only `qa.html` counter, while retaining original busy/error/visible screenshot evidence; no real device revoked or private user metadata. Run this one focused Settings browser suite and inspect screenshots only after source CI passes. No full release/Windows/WebKit/Android/production QA at Draft. New source head unvalidated; don't count I18.05 or claim the browser proof yet.

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 99/132 completed (unchanged; accepted I19.03 boundary is being extended, not recounted).** Exact prior head `440b708` [CI #44537663235](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/44537663235) validate/CodeQL/Unified SUCCESS and strict 528.0 KiB main-JS limit PASS. ## 2026-11-19 — I19.03 same-transaction local receipt quota and stale-update race

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 99/132 completed (unchanged; I19.03 previously accepted, this is a source-supported adjacent defect class).** Earlier exact `22e9b87` CI #44530723102 **validate SUCCESS**, including actual disposable PostgreSQL changed-event+configuration-cent matrix, root/API npm audit, tests, TypeScript/build/strict privacy/bundle; CodeQL/Unified were still in progress at that inspection and must not be claimed accepted without result.

**Confirmed local-only IndexedDB race:** `createReceiptDraft` read 30-draft/60-MiB quota in a readonly transaction and inserted later in a separate readwrite transaction; two concurrent captures (including different tabs) could each pass stale quota. `saveReceiptProposal` and `markReceiptDraftError` likewise read before a separate write and could recreate a receipt deleted between operations. Move each read/check/write into its **one serialized IndexedDB readwrite transaction** with unchanged count/byte caps, error copy, OCR privacy/no-upload semantics and finance-then-local-cleanup contract. Remove the now-unused independent read helper. Narrow regression source locks create quota and both update/delete race boundaries. No new capture UX, no synthetic finance writes or external data. **New source candidate not validated on head**; exact source/CodeQL/Unified needed, bounded receipt runtime/browser verification when final coherent QA is run. ## 2026-11-18 — I19.01 rapid row-identity correction for multi-source purchases and split expenses (source/browser candidate)

**Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 99/132 completed (unchanged: I19.01 remains PARTIAL; I19.02 and I19.07 already accepted).** Previous exact commit `7be4f737` [CI #44372133966](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/44372133966) **SUCCESS**: 1,950/1,950 Vitest, TS/build/privacy/bundle/local PostgreSQL, CodeQL/Unified and both root/API mandatory npm audits zero vulnerabilities on that head. Draft opt-in off, no repeated card validation.

**Confirmed same-owner defect:** Quick Entry assigned newly added multi-funding rows `funding-${Date.now()}` and new split subparts `p${Date.now()}`. Rapid Add actions in the same millisecond could create duplicate React keys and, critically, a shared `row.id` in the funding update map and split raw-amount text map. Editing one funding row could change both; a split row's raw amount could appear in another row. That can misrepresent an owner-entered purchase draft before validation even though the canonical multi-funding engine rejects duplicate source IDs.

**Bounded correction:** One instance-local monotonic draft row allocator, reused by both editor sections, allocates outside React functional state updaters and skips IDs already present (including imported split part IDs). No finance event IDs, canonical ledger legs, economic-event counts, backend/schema/auth/Storage or edit-provenance contracts are changed. Dedicated source regression locks both consumers and the elimination of Date.now-derived row IDs. The existing `action-center-context` Chromium suite simulates two rapid same-timestamp Add clicks in each editor, edits the third of four rows, requires the fourth remain independent, confirms zero posted finance events, and captures 375px mobile screenshots with no document overflow. Existing invalid-cent and focus/error QA remains intact.

**Validation boundary:** new source, QA and tests NOT YET PASSED on this candidate; one targeted existing focused browser QA (prefer `action-center-context` alone) and direct inspection of the two mobile images/logs required before acceptance. No increment until entire I19.01 scope accepted; final Ready WebKit/Windows/Lighthouse/recovery and standalone dependency PR #660 reconciliation pending.

**Integration/release:** Draft PR #654 `fix/649-bulk-remediation` → `develop`; no merge/main/production, hosted DB/Storage, migration or Android changes. **Next:** inspect automatic exact-head source CI once, enable one scoped action-center-context QA using existing Draft opt-in or narrow selector, accept only after logs/screenshots; keep opt-in OFF again and proceed to next unblocked #649 source work in a coherent batch.

## 2026-11-16 — I03.04/I03.06 stronger runtime provenance and PIN/device timing regression (no duplicate count)

**Progress: Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 90/132 completed (unchanged).** The I03.04 and I03.06 **K verified-existing** contract units were already accepted and counted in the 2026-11-11 I03 authentication checkpoint (~61/132). The following additional security tests strengthen those same units rather than constituting two newly completed sub-implementations. Do **not** inflate counts to 92/132.

**I03.04 additional handler-runtime coverage:** Added `tests/649-profile-session-provenance.test.ts` (7 tests) for the real rewritten `/api/auth/session` account/device delegates and login/MFA/logout entrypoints. It proves browser profile/device/session routes reject bearer-only sessions (never substitute an Authorization header for a cookie), AAL1 profile/device mutations fail 403 while legitimate MFA-pending session status remains available, AAL2 nonowner 401 clears only the actual rejected cookie, missing/cross-site Origin blocks authorized cookie writes, and when a valid owner cookie plus invalid Authorization header coexist the browser-only profile/device routes still use the **cookie** token. Login, enrollment, verification and logout reject absent Origin before upstream calls; production host-only `__Host-` cookies retain Secure, HttpOnly, SameSite=Strict, Path=/ and no Domain. No production Auth, API, cookie policy, privileges or finance code changed. [Exact source CI #43827991064](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/43827991064) **SUCCESS** (315/315 files, 1,784/1,784 tests, TypeScript/build/privacy/bundle, both disposable PostgreSQL smoke suites, CodeQL and Unified).

**I03.06 additional timing/active-device proof:** Added `tests/649-pin-device-timing.test.ts` (7 parameterized tests) against the actual `requireSession` pipeline and local synthetic Auth/ticket responses. For cookie and native bearer sessions, GoTrue user auth must precede the **active-device registry**, which precedes the server-held PIN-ticket RPC. A revoked device is denied 401 DEVICE_ACCESS_REVOKED **without consulting even a syntactically valid previously accepted PIN ticket**; only an actual cookie session is cleared, never an unrelated browser cookie for an explicit bearer. A still-active device with expired PIN lease receives 423 APP_LOCK_REQUIRED without being logged out. Rechecking the same apparently valid bearer+ticket after device revocation is again denied before PIN validation. This complements the previously accepted real disposable SQL App Lock ACL/owner+AAL2/active-device proof and source/UI lock-on-idle/visibility regressions; it is not a separate production/PIN rollout or fresh browser gate. [Exact source CI #43828740207](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/43828740207) **SUCCESS** (316/316 files, 1,791/1,791 tests, TypeScript/build/privacy/bundle, disposable App Lock/receivable PG, CodeQL and Unified).

**Safety/integration:** Draft [PR #654](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/pull/654) → `develop` remains unmerged. No hosted Supabase changes, TOTP bypass, real PIN/password, destructive data operations, `main`, Android edits or production release. The additive dated-receivable migration remains **release-PENDING**. Previous I03.05 200% zoom/TOTP Chromium evidence remains accepted; no new user-visible feature was changed, so previous screenshot QA was not redundantly rerun. Full Ready-head rendered/WebKit/Lighthouse/Windows/isolated recovery integration gates remain separate.

## 2026-11-14 — I18.03 ACCEPTED: server-revision-aware Windows backup freshness with verified responsive QA

**Progress: Implementations 6/23 completed · Sub-implementations 89/132 completed (+1 I18.03).**

**I18.03 ACCEPTED (+1 at repository/automated UI scope):** [Exact source/security CI #43643304785](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/43643304785) **SUCCESS**, including validate, CodeQL, Unified, TypeScript/build, hard privacy/bundle limits and local PostgreSQL security/chronology smoke. [Single focused Windows backup Chromium #43643560842](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/43643560842) **SUCCESS**: 13/13 backup-specific Vitest, 18 responsive synthetic native bridge states across **1440px desktop, 768px tablet and 375px mobile**, three error-to-retry screenshots, two dark-mode states, corrupt-only alert, validated restore confirmation and full syntax/build checks. Downloaded immutable artifact **#14132628547** and directly inspected `desktop-backup-stale-revision-desktop.png`, `-tablet.png`, `-mobile.png`, and `desktop-backup-restore-confirm-desktop.png`. The new recent-but-older-revision copy is fully visible and nonoverlapping at all three widths, without new horizontal overflow. Original corrupt restore stays disabled and destructive import requires explicit confirmation; no actual restore executed.

**Confirmed gap and correction:** Before this patch the Windows status IPC read fresh owner+AAL2+PIN-authorized `/api/data` merely to authorize the request, discarding its **current finance revision**; a locally SHA-256-verified but outdated backup could appear as fully current for 24 hours after newer confirmed finance mutations. Now `desktop/main.cjs` returns that authoritative revision in the existing authenticated `myfinhub:backup-status` reply; `desktop/backup-store.cjs` reports the revision of the **latest valid** SHA-checked snapshot, including fallback if a newer file is damaged. The backward-compatible bridge types expose both values and the existing `DesktopBackupPanel` renders **«Έγκυρο αντίγραφο παλαιότερης έκδοσης»** / **«Δεν περιλαμβάνει ακόμη τις νεότερες οικονομικές αλλαγές.»** for a recent valid backup whose revision differs. The status remains informational: a verified older file is **not corrupt**, while a failed/corrupt/overdue state retains the existing distinct warning. A new synthetic recent backup r465 vs current server r466 proves the exact case across three viewports. Timestamp validation, future-clock rollback defense, backup SHA/restore validation, revision tracking, 45-file/30-day retention, folder change preserving old backups and owner-protected IPC all remain covered by existing tests.

**Operational boundaries:** `src/hooks/useDesktopAutoBackup.ts` is **unchanged**: successful local backup is still scheduled by last valid timestamp and the existing 24-hour daily policy, not triggered on every new finance revision. No backup history rewrite, actual Windows installed-app run, production backup, live database change or destructive restore. Full Ready-head installer/Windows lifecycle/rendered/WebKit/performance/security matrix and any approved hosted disaster-recovery proof remain separate. The Draft-only `<!-- run-desktop-backup-focused-qa -->` marker was disabled after the successful run. No overlap or merge with #653 FinanceData backup-scope copy, #651 Transactions cash signs, or #650 draft audit plan. PR #654 remains Draft → `develop`, unmerged; `main`, Android, hosted finance and private credentials untouched.

**Next:** Continue the next highest-priority unblocked #649 P0 work (do not re-audit accepted I03.03/I18.03/I22.01/02/03/05/06/07); stage coherent independent changes and only then the full Ready-head gate/integration once remaining plan scope warrants it. Retain I22.04 leaked-password hosted Auth setting as separate owner-approved operational action.

## 2026-11-10 — I17.05 exact page-entry focus gating candidate after second browser regression (latest)

**Progress: Implementations 5/23 completed · Sub-implementations 50/132 completed (unchanged).** [Exact source CI #42816655699](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/42816655699) **SUCCESS** at `3e186e31f33577e7db21f47cae5c72198725670e`, but [combined Chromium #42817253108](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/42817253108) **PARTIAL FAILURE**: `icon-packs` PASS, `taxonomy-management` PASS, `command-palette` FAIL again at exact Lending keyboard focus after the named rule editor and Reports link scenarios had passed. The actual selected Lending row was `aria-pressed=true` and search modal gone, but `document.activeElement===body`. A dialog-unmount observer alone is not sufficient: AppShell also uses `AnimatePresence mode="wait"` with a new route entering at opacity 0→1. A focus attempt before both animations finish can be lost.

**New precise candidate:** `LendingPage` now checks actual command dialog absence **and** that the exact selected row is rendered inside an AppShell route whose computed opacity is at least 0.999 before focusing it. A short-lived `MutationObserver` plus bounded animation-frame readiness checks wait on visible state instead of fixed arbitrary delays; all frames and observer are cancelled on cleanup. The original strict focus assertion stays unchanged. Browser QA adds capped focus event diagnostics and route opacity on failure; takes rule editor screenshots only after exiting search modal is genuinely absent (first previous capture showed an interim translucent overlap; the second stable capture was correct). No finance/model/API/security/RLS change, no CSS budget change. **Pending exact new-head CI/CodeQL/Unified then one smaller Draft `<!-- run-649-rules-icon-qa -->` focused `command-palette icon-packs` run**. The independent taxonomy QA and 320px picker passed twice, so do not repeat `taxonomy-management` for an unrelated Lending-only focus correction. **I17.05 remains NOT counted** until the full Command Palette suite passes.

**Parallel independent I18.05/I18.03 read-only findings:** Existing `tests/device-access.test.ts` covers owner+AAL2, RLS active-session denial, hidden revoked-row registration race, origin and correct successful/failed receipt ordering; `scripts/settings-tabs-qa.mjs` exercises remote Android device revoke busy/failure/visible error. **Progress: Implementations 5/23 completed · Sub-implementations 46/132 completed (no change).** Corrected QA-only reorder wait at `acc8d271fed52258209a27fb8b5b7b526d48cbc5` passed exact [CI/CodeQL/Unified #42797985892](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/42797985892), but subsequent [focused Chromium #42798470835](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/42798470835) still **FAIL** at the exact reordered index. The genuine root cause is a **product double-application bug**: `App/qa.updateTaxonomy` applies `applyTaxonomyOperation` once to FinanceData, then `SettingsPage.runTaxonomyOperation` replayed `taxonomyOperationPreview(draftRef.current,operation)` after the receipt and after the data update had already synchronized the draft, potentially moving one item twice or attempting a duplicate category addition. This violates a revision-confirmed single operation. Fix removes post-ack replay, retains the existing authoritative `useEffect([data.state.settings])` syncing drafts, awaits the original durable server receipt, and retains the one-flight lock/ARIA busy plus failure preservation. Adds a source regression guard against reintroducing the replay; no backend/migration/RLS/Android/production changes. Existing taxonomy test insists exact single-step reorder (not weakened), and pending/rejected synthetic browser checks will now run after it. The `icon-packs` suite separately **PASS** on both candidate heads and direct-approved desktop/mobile screenshots from artifact #13754811521 were inspected. **Neither I17.03 nor I17.04 is counted pending exact new source CI and successful focused `icon-packs taxonomy-management` browser evidence**. ## 2026-11-10 — I16.04 ambiguous provider asset registration safety (candidate, validation pending)

**Progress: Implementations 5/23 completed · Sub-implementations 44/132 completed (unchanged).** Existing provider logo upload uses owner+AAL2/RLS-bound Storage POST then a separate invoker registration RPC. Prior failure handling unconditionally deleted the just-uploaded Storage object on any RPC error, even if an upstream error occurred *after commit*. This can destroy a legitimately registered file. Conversely if the registration definitively fails and Storage DELETE also fails, an orphan may remain. This is a confirmed two-store receipt/reconciliation failure class.

**Candidate source/guard batch:** in `server/accountMetadataStore.ts`, use exact random asset-key owner-scoped PostgreSQL registry read-back after ambiguous/failed registration response. If the verified existing row matches provider, role, variant, bucket and precise path, return that acknowledged asset without DELETE; if the registry is positively empty, attempt at most two Storage DELETEs for **only this newly generated upload** and propagate the original registration error once cleanup succeeds. If registry reconciliation is unavailable/malformed/mismatched, fail closed with `PROVIDER_ASSET_REGISTRATION_UNVERIFIED` and do **not** delete a possibly committed object. If confirmed-absent cleanup cannot be acknowledged, return explicit `PROVIDER_ASSET_CLEANUP_PENDING` and bounded server diagnostics. No blanket provider-asset purge or historical provider mutation. `tests/provider-brand-management.test.ts` now covers confirmed-absent cleanup, committed-after-503 reconciliation, unreadable registry (zero DELETE), transient cleanup failure with bounded retry. **Source validation/CodeQL REQUIRED on exact new head; do not count I16.04 complete** until outcomes are observed, and remain explicit that persistent orphan recovery/asset-binding retry/partial-success and hosted storage are separate acceptance needs.

**Execution boundary:** PR #654 remains Draft; no UI/CSS, no RLS/migration or finance edits, no Android, production DB/Storage action, merge, release, or expensive full Ready matrix. Use narrow source CI and existing provider focused browser only if user-visible behavior changes or its contracts are affected. After passing CI, reconcile the current checkpoint and remaining I16.04 orphan lifecycle plan.

## 2026-11-10 — I19.07 opaque finance-entry shell fix after focused browser evidence (historical)

**Authoritative source progress: Implementations 5/23 completed · Sub-implementations 36/132 completed.** Source [CI #42681032376](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/42681032376) SUCCESS (CodeQL/Unified gate, source tests, CSS below unchanged hard limit). **Root cause established by CSS/theme evidence (not an animation race):** `src/styles/mobile-reports-settings-editors.css` used `var(--surface-translucent)!important` for mobile `.quick-modal`, header and footer. Production theme tokens deliberately define that role as `rgba(255,255,255,.94)` (light) and `rgba(20,30,48,.96)` (dark). In synthetic QA documents without production theme bootstrap, the missing custom property can make the important background declaration invalid/transparent. The generic and contextual finance-entry forms therefore had an inappropriate glass surface; merely waiting for Framer Motion cannot make it opaque. **Patch the common mobile owner**, not isolated page-specific styles: replace all three background declarations with opaque semantic `var(--surface-elevated,var(--surface))`, respecting both themes and a safe QA fallback. Add source guards and browser assertions for final opacity of modal and header, preserve the existing ancestor-opacity threshold >= .995, 390px geometry and existing 320px large-text light/dark sibling screenshots. Do not weaken assertions, remove visual checks, or increase CSS hard budget. This is an **I19.07 code/QA candidate pending exact-head CI/CodeQL and focused Chromium acceptance**; not counted until evidence is inspected. PR #654 remains Draft; marker `<!-- run-649-quick-layout-qa -->` retained for one exact-head browser rerun, then remove. No production/Android/merge.

- Previous draft head `c15655a5a3dde92a37daab858b513a1f1acf9490`: [CI #41032708411](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/41032708411) **SUCCESS**, [CodeQL #41032708343](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/41032708343) **SUCCESS**, [focused QA #41032708153](https://github.com/MariosGiannakaras/MyFinHub/actions/runs/41032708153) **SUCCESS**. - Source-confirmed failure class in local clipboard copy: CanonicalCreditCardStack ignored a false legacy `execCommand('copy')` return, and a thrown copy command could leave a temporary sensitive textarea in DOM. AccountIban's legacy route also lacked guaranteed cleanup when copy threw. Introduced `src/lib/copyTextWithFallback.ts` as a shared explicit user-action copy contract. Native clipboard success returns immediately; denied/unavailable native API tries a single in-document fallback; `false` and exceptions are failures; the DOM node's value is erased and node removed in `finally`. No logging or persistence, no vault read/write or finance mutation changed.
- Applied shared copy helper to CanonicalCreditCardStack, InteractivePaymentCard and AccountIban. Canonical stack now distinguishes live success `role=status` vs failure `role=alert`; InteractivePaymentCard uses shared OperationFeedback success/error tone; AccountIban publishes an accessible failure without revealing the copied content. Added `tests/649-clipboard-copy.test.ts` with simulated native success, native denial + legacy success, false and throw failures, select exception and consumer source contracts.
- Expanded the **single** existing draft-only opt-in focused QA suite to include `card-vault-runtime` and `account-metadata` in the same batch as contextual, receipts, Settings and Savings; no duplicate workflow/full matrix. ## 2026-11-05 — I18.04 stale-client import revision gap; compatibility-safe atomic guard staged
- Source-reproduced **P0 import precondition gap**: existing `src/lib/api.ts importData` supplied confirmation but no `If-Match`, `api/import.ts` called `writeStore(body, undefined, true)`, `server/storage.ts` selected `rpc/rheomiq_import_state` without expected revision, and the latest 2026-09-30 relational finance cutover still defined `rheomiq_import_state(p_data jsonb)` without an expected revision. The previous synthetic 409 pre-commit rejection **did not prove** real server optimistic conflict protection. A stale web/Windows session could therefore replace newer finance after another session advanced revision, despite its local destructive confirmation. Preserve existing pre-import backup and explicit confirmation; do not call this risk a demonstrated production incident.
- **Coherent staged fix:** new *additive* PostgreSQL `rheomiq_import_state_checked(p_data,p_expected_revision)` SECURITY INVOKER function, owner/AAL2 access check and `FOR UPDATE` revision comparison within the **same transaction** before delegating to the established relational finance import RPC. Prior backup/history/ledger/audit logic is reused verbatim. A genuine mismatch fails with existing `REVISION_CONFLICT` 409 and has **zero import commits**. New web `useFinance` sends its last acknowledged `revisionRef.current` as HTTP `If-Match`, route selects the checked RPC when present. The historical header-less API/RPC remain unchanged for native/Android compatibility; **legacy callers still lack this new conflict guarantee**, requiring separate coordinated protocol update before a mandatory server-side If-Match policy. No Android code change.
- Narrow source regression verifies API authorization/confirmation, guarded path, SQL lock-before-import order, no `SECURITY DEFINER`, unchanged legacy call, and actual real-hook Chromium scenario that advances the synthetic server revision between read and import without using the forced-409 fixture. Browser must assert rejected 409, zero import commits/replays, red conflict, Undo/Redo blocked and read-only recovery. Previously accepted 409/503 and postcommit history-failure cases remain. **New migration and behavior are SOURCE-STAGED ONLY**; require exact-head CI/CodeQL, History 3-suite rendered artifact and direct image review, plus isolated PostgreSQL migration/transaction rollback verification before database/release acceptance. Production migration/deploy is **not authorized or performed**.
- **Implementations 0/23 completed · Sub-implementations 8/132 completed**. I18.04 remains PARTIAL; I18.02 independent DB+Storage restore still unproven. PR #654 stays Draft, main/develop/production/Android untouched. Temporarily turn History focused opt-in ON for this new coherent implementation head. Do not claim the source guard proves that production Supabase applied the migration or that legacy native imports are protected.
